---
title: Host actions
description: Generated reference for every host Action available through the Plugin Actions service.
---

{/* Generated from the canonical ActionSpec registry. Do not hand-edit. */}

Use this reference when calling host Actions from a Plugin invocation context. It is generated from the canonical ActionSpec registry, so each listed id is available through `context.services.actions.execute(...)` and no hand-maintained Action list can drift from the host.

```ts
const result = await context.services.actions.execute('action.spec.get', {
  id: 'session.status.get',
}, {
  signal: context.signal,
});
```

The Action service validates the exact input and result contract for each id. Use the input guidance below when it is present; the TypeScript API remains the final typed contract.

Every row carries its canonical **Caller authority** from the ActionSpec registry. Being listed here proves the Action is discoverable on the Plugin surface; it does not imply a plugin caller can satisfy it. A row marked `present_user` is admitted only when the host stamps present-user caller authority — a plugin or API-token caller carries automation authority and receives the typed `present_user_required` failure instead of a result. Caller authority is host-stamped: Action input can never supply, widen, or narrow it.

In 0.3 development source, client-placed host Actions invoked through an admitted daemon host reach one connected app through the existing machine reverse channel. The app preserves the admitted surface and authority and uses its current mounted owners. Missing connected handlers return typed unavailable; a lost response after dispatch returns `outcome_uncertain` without automatic retry. Standalone CLI availability remains disabled. Plugin-declared client Actions retain their separate artifact and plugin-occurrence dispatch contract. See [Actions, tools and commands](/plugins/api/actions-tools-commands#host-actions-that-run-in-a-connected-app).

Authority comes from the admitted invocation, never from Session content. A plugin running on someone's Session runtime keeps its own host-stamped principal no matter who wrote the latest message: the newest input author is correlation, and neither a `requestedBy`-style field nor input authorship delegates that person's Account. Host operations a plugin performs on a Session are recorded with the actual admitted execution Account plus Agent producer provenance, so an Agent post is visibly Agent-produced rather than attributed to the person who asked for it. Confirmation is a separate decision from authorization: an approval, or an explicit user waiver where the Action policy permits one, can satisfy the Action's confirmation requirement, but it never grants missing Session access, surface enablement, or endpoint consent.

Bundled and externally installed plugins reach these Actions through the same `activate(api)` ABI, the same public SDK services seam, and the same Account Action settings. Trust is a user decision, not a packaging one: there is no smaller allowlist for an external plugin and no host-internal import that makes a first-party example work. Two surface distinctions are worth knowing before you pick a call site:

- `session.message.send` accepts an optional `recipient`. Omit it for the main Session; `{ kind: 'execution_run', runId }` addresses one Session-owned run through the Session's own Pending queue and target admission. The separate `execution.run.send` Action is only for a **detached** run with `sessionId: null`.
- The trusted plugin `userText` binding additionally carries manifest-declared attachments and host-stamped source provenance. The public API-Token/SDK binding for the same Action is intentionally strict and smaller: it rejects `source`, attachments, structured launch and `idempotencyKey` rather than accepting them as untyped JSON. That is an authority boundary, not a reduced capability set. See [Actions, tools and commands](/plugins/api/actions-tools-commands#calling-host-actions).

## `account.apiTokens.create`

**Create API token** — Create a named API token for the current Account, optionally with trusted-device-prepared encryption access. The token secret is shown once.

- Safety: `danger`; side effect: `danger`.
- Approval: `required` result; `blocking` flow.
- Caller authority: `present_user` — only a host-stamped present-user caller is admitted; plugin and API-token callers receive `present_user_required`.
- Also surfaced on: `ui`, `agent`, `mcp`, `cli`.

### Input guidance

- `label` (text, required): Label
- `expiresAt` (text): Expiry

## `action.options.resolve`

**Resolve action options** — Resolve valid options for an action field, including dynamic options sources.

- Safety: `safe`; side effect: `read`.
- Approval: `required` result; `blocking` flow.
- Caller authority: `account_automation` — automation callers (including trusted plugins and API Tokens) are admitted.
- Also surfaced on: `ui`, `voice`, `agent`, `mcp`, `cli`, `rpc`, `api`.

### Input guidance

- `actionId` (text): Action id — Optional when optionsSourceId is provided directly.
- `fieldPath` (text): Field path — Dot-path for the action input field.
- `optionsSourceId` (text): Options source id — Direct options source lookup when known.
- `sessionId` (text): Session id — Omit to use the current invoking Session. Set only for an intentional authorized cross-Session target.
- `draftInput` (json): Partial action input — Partial input for dependent options, for example &#123;"backendTargetKeys":["agent:pi"]&#125; when resolving a model, configuration, or connected service.
- `query` (text): Query filter — Optional search text to filter the returned options.
- `limit` (text): Limit — Maximum number of options to return.

- `actionId` (text, required): Action id

## `execution.run.start`

**Start execution run** — Start a new execution run. Set waitForCompletion=true to wait for a terminal run result. waitTimeoutSeconds only bounds this observation; it never stops, retries, or starts the run. A nonempty sessionId selects that exact authorized Session. Omitting sessionId inherits the current Action Session, or selects detached scope when none exists. sessionId:null selects detached scope even inside a current Session.

- Safety: `safe`; side effect: `write`.
- Approval: `optional` result; `deferred` flow.
- Caller authority: `account_automation` — automation callers (including trusted plugins and API Tokens) are admitted.
- Also surfaced on: `ui`, `voice`, `agent`, `mcp`, `cli`, `rpc`, `api`.

### Input guidance

- `sessionId` (text): Session id — Use a nonempty value for an exact authorized Session. Omit it to inherit the current Action Session, or to select detached scope when no current Session exists. Set null to select detached scope even inside a current Session.
- `intent` (text, required): Intent
- `backendTarget` (textarea, required): Backend target (json)
- `instructions` (textarea): Instructions
- `permissionMode` (select, required): Permission intent — Permission intent. Preferred values: read_only | default | auto | yolo. Compatible aliases are accepted.
- `retentionPolicy` (text, required): Retention policy
- `runClass` (text, required): Run class
- `ioMode` (text, required): IO mode
- `waitForCompletion` (boolean): Wait for completion — Return the terminal run disposition under wait instead of returning immediately after start.
- `waitTimeoutSeconds` (text): Wait timeout seconds — Optional observation deadline; requires waitForCompletion=true and never stops the run.
- `initialContextMode` (text): Initial context mode
- `modelId` (text): Model id — Optional model for the run backend (same vocabulary as session spawn). Omit for the backend default.
- `configOptions` (json): Config options (e.g. reasoning effort) — Optional agent config-option overrides, e.g. &#123;"reasoning_effort":"high"&#125;. Merged into sessionConfigOptionOverrides at the boundary; a conflict fails with invalid_parameters.
- `connectedServices` (textarea): Connected services (json) — Optional connected-services selection for the run backend. Accepts "native" (suppress all connected-service inheritance), a per-service string ("&lt;service&gt;:group:&lt;id&gt;", "&lt;service&gt;:&lt;profileId&gt;", "&lt;service&gt;:native"), an array, or the full object; omitted = session-spawn defaulting (literal). Enumerate valid selections via the shared session-spawn options source.
- `secretReferenceOverlay` (json): Saved Secret overrides (json) — Optional value-free Saved Secret references for this launch only. Values are resolved by the exact daemon and the selected Profile is not changed.

## `execution.run.stop`

**Stop execution run** — Stop one execution run in the selected scope. A nonempty sessionId selects that exact authorized Session. Omitting sessionId inherits the current Action Session, or selects detached scope when none exists. sessionId:null selects detached scope even inside a current Session.

- Safety: `safe`; side effect: `write`.
- Approval: `optional` result; `deferred` flow.
- Caller authority: `account_automation` — automation callers (including trusted plugins and API Tokens) are admitted.

## `execution.run.wait`

**Wait for execution run** — Observe an execution run until terminal by default, or select permission attention, either condition, or a changed snapshot. Pass timeoutSeconds to bound the observation; omit it for no Happier-side deadline. Timeout only ends this observation; it does not stop, retry, or start the run. Cancellation only ends this wait. A nonempty sessionId selects that exact authorized Session. Omitting sessionId inherits the current Action Session, or selects detached scope when none exists. sessionId:null selects detached scope even inside a current Session.

- Safety: `safe`; side effect: `read`.
- Approval: `required` result; `blocking` flow.

- `runId` (text, required): Run id
- `timeoutSeconds` (text): Timeout seconds (optional)

## `machines.access.grant.set`

**Share a machine** — Save or change a current person, Team or group grant. Can manage includes sharing and owner-equivalent machine administration. People with terminal or agent access to this machine run as your operating-system user and can obtain your local credentials and Account encryption key, if present. No Happier API reads or exports owner credentials or keys; anything a shared shell prints can contain them. We recommend sharing a dedicated team machine.

- Safety: `danger`; side effect: `danger`.
- Approval: `required` result; `blocking` flow.
- Caller authority: `account_automation` — automation callers (including trusted plugins and API Tokens) are admitted.
- Also surfaced on: `ui`, `cli`, `agent`, `mcp`, `voice`, `api`.

### Input guidance

- `serverId` (text, required): Home ID
- `machineId` (text, required): Machine ID
- `principal` (json, required): Person, Team or group
- `level` (select, required): Access

### Input guidance

- `engineIds` (multiselect, required): Review engines — Select one or more engines. Each engine runs as its own execution run.
- `outputs` (multiselect): Also write a walkthrough
- `comparisonId` (text): Captured comparison — Saved exact comparison identity required when requesting a walkthrough.
- `narrator.engineId` (select): Narrator — Optional explicit capable engine; defaults to the first selected capable reviewer.
- `narrator.modelId` (text): Narrator model
- `instructions` (textarea, required): Instructions — What you want the reviewers to focus on.
- `teamCredentialModel` (json): Team credential model (json) — Optional exact Team credential resource/model selection from the recipient-safe Team catalog. Requires one review engine.
- `secretReferenceOverlay` (json): Saved Secret overrides (json) — Optional value-free Saved Secret references for this launch only. Values are resolved by the exact daemon and the selected Profile is not changed.
- `changeType` (select, required): Change type — Which changes to review.
- `base.kind` (select, required): Base selection — How to define the review base for engines that need it.
- `base.baseBranch` (text): Base branch — Branch name to diff against (when base.kind=branch).
- `base.baseCommit` (text): Base commit — Commit SHA to diff against (when base.kind=commit).

## `session.terminals.close`

**Close session terminal** — Operate on the invoking mounted client terminal pane for a Home-qualified Session or an accepted requester Project checkout. Open, split and run_script return a pane member intent, not a ready shell. Restart requests the mounted controller, not a completed process restart. The Machine open/restart Actions own physical launch approval with the original caller provenance. Project shells retain their exact accepted workspace. A headless host returns unsupported_action. Closing stops owned shell terminals; attached agent terminals and borrowed views are only removed from the pane.

- Safety: `danger`; side effect: `danger`.
- Approval: `required` result; `blocking` flow.
- Caller authority: `account_automation` — automation callers (including trusted plugins and API Tokens) are admitted.
- Also surfaced on: `ui`, `voice`, `agent`, `mcp`, `cli`, `api`.

### Input guidance

- `scopeId` (text, required): Home-qualified Session or Project scope
- `terminalId` (text, required): Terminal id

Closing stops owned shell terminals; attached agent terminals and borrowed views are only removed from the pane.

## `subagents.delegate.start`

**Start delegate run** — No additional description is published.

- Safety: `safe`; side effect: `external`.
- Approval: `optional` result; `deferred` flow.
- Caller authority: `account_automation` — automation callers (including trusted plugins and API Tokens) are admitted.
- Also surfaced on: `ui`, `voice`, `agent`, `mcp`, `cli`, `api`.

### Input guidance

- `backendTargetKeys` (multiselect, required): Agent backend targets — Select Agent backend targets for Happier-managed runs; use repeated launches or Agent-native subagents for homogeneous parallelism capacity, not parallelism capacity in this field.
- `instructions` (textarea, required): Instructions — What you want the delegate(s) to do.
- `permissionMode` (select): Permission mode — Permission intent. Preferred values: read_only | default | auto | yolo. Compatible aliases are accepted.
- `modelId` (text): Model id — Optional model applied to every started run (same vocabulary as session spawn). Omit for the backend default.
- `teamCredentialModel` (json): Team credential model (json) — Optional exact Team credential resource/model selection from the recipient-safe Team catalog. Requires one Agent backend target.
- `secretReferenceOverlay` (json): Saved Secret overrides (json) — Optional value-free Saved Secret references for this launch only. Values are resolved by the exact daemon and the selected Profile is not changed.
- `configOptions` (json): Config options (e.g. reasoning effort) — Optional agent config-option overrides applied to every started run, e.g. &#123;"reasoning_effort":"high"&#125;. Merged canonically; a conflict with sessionConfigOptionOverrides fails.
- `connectedServices` (json): Connected services (json) — Optional blanket connected-services selection for every target. Use "native" to suppress all connected-service inheritance. An exact connectedServicesByBackendTargetKey entry overrides it.
- `connectedServicesByBackendTargetKey` (textarea): Connected services per target (json) — Optional connected-services selection per backend target key. Accepts "native" (suppress all connected-service inheritance), a per-service string ("&lt;service&gt;:group:&lt;id&gt;", "&lt;service&gt;:&lt;profileId&gt;", "&lt;service&gt;:native"), an array, or the full object; omitted targets use session-spawn defaulting (literal). Enumerate valid selections via the shared session-spawn options source.

## `workflow.authoring.conversation.bind`

**Bind Workflow conversation** — Bind a current existing Session to a step (or the default with stepId null) in the answering mounted Workflow draft. This authors one change; it does not save, start the Workflow or submit text.

- Safety: `safe`; side effect: `external`.
- Approval: `required` result; `blocking` flow.
- Caller authority: `account_automation` — automation callers (including trusted plugins and API Tokens) are admitted.
- Also surfaced on: `ui`, `agent`, `mcp`, `api`.

### Input guidance

- `scope` (json, required): Home and Account
- `draftId` (text, required): Mounted Workflow draft id
- `stepId` (json, required): Agent step id (null for Workflow default)
- `address` (json, required): Qualified Session address
